German national focus tree. Clicking on the branches leads to the appropriate section.

# Develop Modern Maneuver Warfare Branch [verified for 1.17]

Army branch of the German national focus tree.
Clicking on a national focus icon leads to the appropriate table row.

Focus:
FOCUS Develop Modern Maneuver Warfare: We must recognize the evolving nature of modern warfare and develop a new doctrine emphasizing the integration of infantry and new technologies to maximize our military effectiveness on the battlefield.
Prerequisites: None
Effects:
Germany:
- cp : +25.0
- army exp +25
- Available as Chief of Army:
 - W. von Brauchitsch (Army Maneuver (Expert):
 - Division speed: +10.00%
 - army exp Gain: +0.30 daily
- If The DLC Arms Against Tyranny is not enabled:
 - Modify Henschel with:
 - Light Tank Production Cost : -5.00%
- Otherwise:
 - Henschel:
 - Gain +1 Size
 - Funds Gain: +25.00%
 - Military Industrial Organization Research Bonus: +5.00%

Focus:
FOCUS Fortify the Vaterland: To secure the German Reich we must construct fortifications wherever needed. Fortifying our defenses will allow us to hold the borders with fewer men, freeing up divisions to participate in any potential offensives elsewhere.
Alternative Descriptions
Prerequisites:
Germany:
- Requires the following:
 - Develop Modern Maneuver Warfare
Effects:
Germany:
- 2x 50% Research bonus for: Fortification Engineering
- Gets the national spirit for 730 day(s):

Spirit Fortify the Vaterland: To secure the German Reich we must construct fortifications wherever needed. Fortifying our defenses will allow us to hold the borders with fewer men, freeing up divisions to participate in any potential offensives elsewhere.
- Land Fort construction speed: +20.00%
- Coastal Fort construction speed: +20.0%

- Unlocks Decision: Fortify the Vaterland

Focus:
FOCUS The Prussian Legacy: 'Where some states have an army, the Prussian Army has a state', Voltaire once said. We will embody this by evolving the old German doctrine of Bewegungskrieg - maneuver warfare - to ensure swift and overwhelming victories on the battlefield.
Prerequisites:
Germany:
- Requires the following:
 - Develop Modern Maneuver Warfare
- ExclusiveM Mutually exclusive with:
 - Adopt New Panzer Doctrine
Effects:
Germany:
- cp : +25.0
- army exp +15
- Add 150 Mastery to all Infantry tracks
- 2x 25% Cost reduction for: Land Doctrine
- Unlocks Decision: Operational Planning, which when activated will grant for 90 days:
 - Add Bewegungskrieg which grants:
 - Infantry Division Attack: +5.0%
 - Breakthrough: +5.00%
- If:
 - The DLC ncns is enabled
 - Is faction leader
 - Add 1 Short Term goal slot to Axis
 - Add faction goal Expand Our Infantry Forces to Axis

Focus:
FOCUS Improve the Motorized Troops: Modern warfare revolves around speed and rapid offensive capabilities. We must ensure that our Schnelltruppen, our motorized units, are ready and capable of leading the charge and delivering a quick and decisive victory.
Prerequisites:
Germany:
- Requires the following:
 - The Prussian Legacy
Effects:
Germany:
- If:
 - Oswald Lutz is active
 - Has not completed focus Reorganize the Wehrmacht
 - Oswald Lutz:
 - Add Skill Level: 1
 - Add Attack Skill Level: 1
 - Add Planning Skill Level: 1
 - Add Logistics Skill Level: 1
- Oswald Lutz Gains Trickster if he does not have the trait
 - Reconnaissance: +25.0%
- Modify the Dynamic Modifier Deutsches Heer with:

Spirit Deutsches Heer: The German Reich has a long and proud military history with a tradition of innovative thinking. The General Staff has sought to improve our armed forces even further since the humiliating defeat in the Great War. Armed with this expertise, our army is poised to strike against any foe who wishes to subjugate our proud people.
- Org Loss when Moving: -10.0%

- Communist :

Spirit Volksarmee: The People’s Army stands as the shield of socialism, ready to protect and spread the proletarian revolution. The innovative thinking of the People’s General Staff ensures that any imperialist aggression will not quench the revolutionary spirit of the German people.
- Org Loss when Moving: -10.0%
- If: The DLC Arms Against Tyranny is not enabled:
 - Modify Alkett with:
 - Light SP Artillery Production Cost : -5.00%
 - Medium SP Artillery Production Cost : -5.00%
 - Heavy SP Artillery Production Cost : -5.00%
- Otherwise:
 - Alkett:
 - Gain +1 Size
 - Funds Gain: +25.00%
 - Military Industrial Organization Research Bonus: +5.00%

Focus:
FOCUS Stormtroopers: The Stosstruppen of the Great War were elite shock troopers meant to lead assaults on the enemy's trenches and create breakthroughs the main infantry could exploit. We should revive this idea and train new elite shock troopers that will epitomize speed, agility, and ruthless efficiency on the battlefield.
Prerequisites:
Germany:
- Requires the following:
 - Artillery Bombardment
Effects:
Germany:

 - Infantry Division Attack: +5.0%

 - Attack: +1
 - Planning: +1
- If he is active in the country, Ernst-Eberhard Hell Gains Brilliant Strategist if he does not have the trait
 - Attack: +1
 - Planning: +1

Focus:
FOCUS Instill Auftragstaktik: Our military has long practiced auftragstaktik, or mission command, emphasizing decentralized command and individual initiative. This has led to flexibility and autonomy, enhancing our ability to adapt and succeed in unforeseen situations. It is paramount that we continue this practice for our forces to be ever-victorious!
Prerequisites:
Germany:
- Requires one of the following:
 - The Prussian Legacy

Focus:
FOCUS Panzergrenadier: Our military doctrine rests on mobility and will depend on how well our motorized troops perform. Neither infantry nor panzer divisions will be successful in their advancements without proper support. By integrating mechanized infantry with our spearheads, we ensure our forces maintain superiority in offensive operations.

Germany:

 - Instill Auftragstaktik

- If has the Dynamic Modifier MEFO Bills modify it with:

Spirit MEFO Bills: MEFO bills were promissory notes created by Hjalmar Schacht to enable the government to fund rearmament, acting through the balance sheet entity Metallurgische Forschungsgesellschaft to hide this rearmament from French and British eyes. These bills must be extended for six-month periods, or their recipients will have to be paid when they fall due.
- Equipment Conversion Speed: +10.0%

- If has the Dynamic Modifier Economy of Conquest modify it with:

Spirit Economy of Conquest: Despite the unfair stipulations of the Versailles Treaty, we rise from the ashes with unwavering determination. Through tireless rearmament, we have committed to forge an unrivaled military industry to fuel our ascent to glory. This will strain our economy, but with victory comes the opportunity to reap the spoils of conquest, channeling the wealth of other nations to strengthen our armed forces. Woe to the vanquished!
- Equipment Conversion Speed: +10.0%

- If has the Dynamic Modifier Recovering Economy modify it with:

Spirit Recovering Economy: Our economy and industry are on the brink of collapse after reckless spending of resources we can ill afford on rearmament. With prudent fiscal policies and sheer determination, we will rebuild our nation and grow stronger than ever before!
- Equipment Conversion Speed: +10.0%

- If has the Dynamic Modifier Wirtschaftswunder modify it with:

Spirit Wirtschaftswunder: We have managed to turn an economy in shambles into one of the world’s leading industrial powers. This remarkable revival can only be described as a Wirtschaftswunder, an economic miracle.
- Equipment Conversion Speed: +10.0%
- Two random German generals will gain the Scavenger trait.

 - 2 random employed army leader that does not have Scavenger:
 - Gains Scavenger
 - Equipment Capture Ratio Gain: +3

FOCUS Adopt New Panzer Doctrine: Heinz Guderian and other radical theorists are pushing for daring new doctrines emphasizing communication, identification of 'schwerpunkte' and local concentrations of combined arms forces. Giving them free reins might help avoid the meat grinder horrors of the Great War.

# Expanding the Luftwaffe Branch [verified for 1.17]

Airforce branch of the German national focus tree.
Clicking on a national focus icon leads to the appropriate table row.

Focus:
FOCUS Expanding the Luftwaffe: Modern wars cannot be won without dominating the skies. We must do everything in our power to develop the air force!

# Prioritize Economic Growth Branch [verified for 1.17]

Slow economic rebuilding branch of the German national focus tree.
Clicking on a national focus icon leads to the appropriate table row.

Focus:
FOCUS Prioritize Economic Growth: Years of negligent fiscal policies have almost ruined our economy. We must build tractors instead of tanks, hand out shovels instead of rifles, and prioritize economic growth instead of growing the arms industry!

Germany:
- ExclusiveM Mutually exclusive with:
 - The Four-Year Plan
- Does not have Modifier: Economy of Conquest
Effects:

 - Fuel Capacity: -25.00%

 - cic construction speed:-30.00%

 - Add 1 Building Slot
 - Add 1 cic
- Available as Political Advisor:
 - Ludwig Erhard (Economic Reformer)
 - Consumer Goods Factories factor: −15%
 - Trade Laws Cost: −33%
 - Economy Laws Cost: −33%

 - has the Dynamic Modifier MEFO Bills
 - Remove as Political Advisor:
 - Hjalmar Schacht
- Political Power : -20
- Gets the national spirit for 60 day(s):

Spirit MEFO Bills Payments: Germany is currently in the process of paying off the MEFO Bills now that they are no longer able to be properly sustained.
- Consumer Goods Factories Factor: 40.0%

- Replace Dynamic Modifier MEFO Bills with:

Spirit Recovering Economy: Our economy and industry are on the brink of collapse after reckless spending of resources we can ill afford on rearmament. With prudent fiscal policies and sheer determination, we will rebuild our nation and grow stronger than ever before!
